before its output root is created. Effective paths at 200 characters require a
verified short alias or staging root first; 230 characters is a hard stop.

prose in manifests instead of path components.

must use the explicit Windows PowerShell 5.1 executable, one quoted scalar
`.ps1` after `-File`, a bounded UTF-8 JSON invocation manifest for complex
values, and a non-mutating target preflight. Arbitrary `%*` forwarding,
`-Command`, `start`, and `Start-Process` wrapper hops are prohibited.

## 2026-08-21 Recovery observation governance and lot 62631-586 capability stop

Recovery intent is now mechanically classified before design or publication.
An `OBSERVE` intent may use only a qualified `STATUS`, `DATA_PULL`, or exact
direct-admin read-only route, and every requested capability must be present in
a pinned route inventory. It cannot install code, launch a helper, act on a
